  {
    "type": "text",
    "text": "# Atlas query playbook — 3.3.0\r\n\r\n## Choose the fact capability\r\n\r\nStandard: monitor/history → pinned report-query/rows. Interactive: explore-query/rows.\r\nThe retained 3.2 long-text endpoints are compatibility-only. Read the active\r\nsection, not all examples. Use live list_entities/list_attributes/tool schemas and\r\nreuse discovery within the same environment. Dates/Vendors below are examples,\r\nnot run defaults. EQL wrapper is `{:entity ... :query ...}`, never Datalog.\r\n\r\nThe examples use plain joins accepted by the observed Studio. They are NOT a\r\nverified paging API. Report3.3 source default/max is1000; compact Top10 bound is221. Other\r\ncollections retain default200/max1000. Older report deployments may default200\r\nand cut off a221-row report unless supported source paging/limit is supplied.\r\nMCP source paging/sorting must use its actually supported schema. Do not paste\r\nparameterized joins into Studio after semantic.studio/query-shape rejection.\r\nIncomplete source results cannot be repaired by paging their truncated handle.\r\n\r\nComplete plain-join projections for monitor/report/history are in\r\n[structured-queries.eql](structured-queries.eql); below are readable examples.\r\nRead the complete example for HISTORICAL_FULL/explicit-target TEST: the short\r\nevent-list projection below alone cannot support Country count/ratio cross-checks.\r\n\r\n## Monitor (standard route)\r\n```clojure\r\n{:entity {:pastdue.monitor-query/calendar-mode \"WEEKDAYS\"\r\n          :pastdue.monitor-query/relative-change-threshold 0.10}\r\n :query [{:pastdue.monitor-query/countries\r\n           [:pastdue.monitor-fact/country-code\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/previous-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/calendar-mode\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/previous-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/previous-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-past-due-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/previous-past-due-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/relative-ratio-change\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/percentage-point-change\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/business-status\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/data-status\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/expected-target-date\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-lag-workdays\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/missing-workdays-between\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/has-comparable-snapshots\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/snapshot-gap-days]}\r\n ]}\r\n```\r\nConsume complete Country facts before locally selecting authorized US/CA. Use\r\ntrusted runtime America/Los_Angeles today, not latest snapshot as a clock.\r\nNormal daily only drills TRIGGERED/ZERO_BASELINE_INCREASE with valid comparable\r\npositive Open and data OK/DELAYED. Test forces applicable valid pairs, warns stale.\r\nAn explicitly requested test/report target uses history-query/comparisons with\r\nrange-start-date=range-end-date=target and the same full Country projection.\r\nThis returns its actual prior eligible snapshot even if the target didn't trigger.\r\nDo not use trigger-events to find nontrigger test pairs or silently substitute\r\nthe latest monitor dates. An arbitrary explicit P/T uses explore PAIR/EXPLICIT.\r\n\r\n## Report (standard route, seven inputs)\r\n```clojure\r\n{:entity {:pastdue.report-query/country-code \"CA\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/target-snapshot-date \"2026-10-26\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/previous-snapshot-date \"2026-10-23\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/calendar-mode \"WEEKDAYS\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/selection-mode \"POSITIVE_TOP_N\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/top-n 10\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/vendor-code 0}\r\n :query [{:pastdue.report-query/rows\r\n           [:pastdue.report-fact/scope-key\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/record-type\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/country-code\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/vendor-code\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/vendor-name\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/calendar-mode\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/selection-mode\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/metric-version\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/business-timezone\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/queried-at-local\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/scope-status\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/dimension\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/eta-value\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/row-kind\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/contribution-rank\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/selected-vendor-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/past-due-count-change\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-country-open\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-country-open\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-country-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-country-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-country-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-country-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-vendor-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-vendor-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-country-contribution\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-country-contribution\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/delta-country-contribution\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/positive-contribution-share\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/selected-positive-coverage\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/display-rank\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/expected-bucket-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/other-bucket-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/increase-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/decrease-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/net-change\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-expected-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-expected-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/counts-match\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/required\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/past-due-count-relative-change\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/expected-record-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/row-ordinal]}\r\n ]}\r\n```\r\nRead one COUNTRY_CONTROL before consuming metrics; it need not be physically\r\nfirst. Then group VENDOR_METRICS and summaries by Country+Vendor+pair, and\r\nVENDOR_CHECK by dimension=code/source/us. BUCKET rank≤5 and OTHER rank6 are\r\nserver summaries. expected-record-count includes the control; raw bucket\r\ncoverage = BUCKET rows + Other's other-bucket-count, not summary row count.\r\nRequired counts-match/total/expected-total checks are mandatory even when empty.\r\nReconcile COUNTRY_CONTROL target/previous-country-past-due-count, -open and\r\n-ratio to the pinned monitor/history facts before combining captures. Drift is\r\nexplicit PARTIAL/UNVERIFIED unless paired raw evidence proves a hard conflict;\r\nqueried-at-local is capture evidence, never a cross-query transaction guarantee.\r\nUS summary is already joint Code=US×Source for EVERY Country. Do not call separate legacy dimension\r\ncollections. SINGLE_VENDOR changes selection-mode, actual vendor-code and top-n=1;\r\nit has no positive-only filter. Pin nearest-eligible P from monitor/history.\r\n\r\n## Flexible analysis: all 20 inputs, explicit denominator and complete projection\r\nThis example asks for all current Vendor×Code shares within each Vendor.\r\n```clojure\r\n{:entity {:pastdue.explore-query/country-code \"CA\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/time-basis \"CURRENT\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/target-snapshot-date \"1970-01-01\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/previous-snapshot-date \"1970-01-01\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/range-start-date \"1970-01-01\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/range-end-date \"1970-01-01\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/calendar-mode \"WEEKDAYS\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/pair-policy \"PREVIOUS_AVAILABLE\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/grain \"VENDOR_CODE\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/vendor-code 0\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/eta-code-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/source-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/department-type-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/master-primary-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/master-manager-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/denominator-scope \"WITHIN_VENDOR\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/ranking-metric \"NONE\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/sort-direction \"ASC\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/change-selection \"ALL\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/top-n 0}\r\n :query [{:pastdue.explore-query/rows\r\n           [:pastdue.explore-fact/scope-key\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/record-type\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/scope-status\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/country-code\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/vendor-code\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/vendor-name\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/calendar-mode\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/time-basis\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/grain\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/eta-code\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/eta-source\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/denominator-scope\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/metric-version\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/organization-basis\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/business-timezone\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/queried-at-local\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-open-count\r\n :pastdue.explore-fact/selection-rank\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/expected-record-count\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/row-ordinal]}\r\n ]}\r\n```\r\nOne CONTROL plus FACT rows, with expected-record-count including CONTROL.\r\nNon-PAIR previous date=1970-01-01 and previous counts are placeholders, not a\r\nzero baseline to diagnose. Sparse control default values are not observations.\r\nUse returned business-timezone/queried-at-local and actual DST label/offset from\r\ntrusted runtime; an ambiguous fall-back local timestamp without its instant/offset\r\nmust not be resolved by guessing. Always project scope-key: JSON tuple of all\r\nrequired inputs, in declaration order. Retain it with original inputs, check exact\r\nconsistency across captured rows/pages; it is not a source revision or watermark.\r\n\r\nResolve other requests by changing the input entity above, preserving all 20\r\ninputs and the same explicit projection (no wildcard or imaginary fields):\r\n\r\n| Intent | Changed inputs |\r\n|---|---|\r\n| Current count Top 10 | grain=VENDOR, denominator-scope=COUNTRY_ALL, ranking-metric=PAST_DUE_COUNT, sort-direction=DESC, top-n=10 |\r\n| Single snapshot | time-basis=SNAPSHOT, target-snapshot-date=2026-10-26; keep inactive previous/range sentinels |\r\n| Exact two dates, declining counts | time-basis=PAIR, target=2026-10-26, previous=2026-10-22, pair-policy=EXPLICIT, grain=VENDOR, denominator=COUNTRY_ALL, ranking=COUNT_CHANGE, direction=ASC, change-selection=NEGATIVE, top-n=10 (use full input names above) |\r\n| Nearest previous, contribution growth | time-basis=PAIR, target-snapshot-date=2026-10-26, previous sentinel, pair-policy=PREVIOUS_AVAILABLE, grain=VENDOR, denominator-scope=COUNTRY_ALL, ranking-metric=CONTRIBUTION_CHANGE, sort-direction=DESC, change-selection=POSITIVE, top-n=10 |\r\n| Daily Country stocks | time-basis=RANGE, range-start-date=2026-10-19, range-end-date=2026-10-26, grain=COUNTRY, denominator-scope=COUNTRY_ALL, ranking-metric=NONE, top-n=0 |\r\n| Vendor joint composition | grain=VENDOR_CODE_SOURCE, vendor-code=31420, denominator-scope=WITHIN_VENDOR, ranking-metric=NONE, top-n=0 |\r\n\r\nThese are illustrative input substitutions, not alternate EQL property aliases.\r\nUse data-contract's exact enums. PAIR-only change selection is not meaningful on\r\nCURRENT/SNAPSHOT/RANGE. WITHIN_VENDOR+COUNTRY is invalid, not silently reinterpreted.\r\n\r\n## Historical events\r\n```clojure\r\n{:entity {:pastdue.history-query/country-code \"US\"\r\n          :pastdue.history-query/calendar-mode \"WEEKDAYS\"\r\n          :pastdue.history-query/range-start-date \"2026-10-01\"\r\n          :pastdue.history-query/range-end-date \"2026-10-26\"\r\n          :pastdue.history-query/relative-change-threshold 0.10}\r\n :query [{:pastdue.history-query/trigger-events\r\n           [:pastdue.history-fact/country-code\r\n            :pastdue.history-fact/target-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.history-fact/previous-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.history-fact/calendar-mode\r\n            :pastdue.history-fact/business-status\r\n            :pastdue.history-fact/data-status\r\n            :pastdue.history-fact/relative-ratio-change]}\r\n ]}\r\n```\r\nFor full historical reports project the discovered Country counts/Open/ratio and\r\nquality fields just as in monitor, in the same history request. Choose latest\r\nactual event per Country after all pages, not first row. Events-only stops here.\r\n\r\n\r\n## Read layer 1: source collection coverage\r\n\r\nProject expected-record-count, row-ordinal, scope-key and full identity for report/\r\nexplore. Use resolver stable ordinal ascending; report3.3 default/max1000, explore default200/max1000. Discover actual\r\nsource paging syntax before use; don't guess an EQL/Studio parameter shape. Keep\r\nrequest scope/projection/order unchanged across pages. Expected counts must agree;\r\nunique identities/ordinals across pages must cover the expected count. A retained\r\nhandle can be a single source page, not the full source population.\r\n\r\nIf the host has no supported way to fetch remaining source records, state the\r\nspecific source gap; don't mark a short final preview as complete or requery each\r\nVendor. The report3.3 default1000 (or supported explicit source limit1000) can contain a compact Top10 report, but does\r\nnot remove result-reader row/cell limits or guarantee cheap source execution.\r\nMonitor/history lack expected-record-count/row-ordinal in their actual schema;\r\nuse actual source pagination/coverage metadata and discovered stable identity/order,\r\nnot invented fields. An incomplete history list cannot establish the latest event.\r\n\r\n## Read layer 2: retained-result materialization\r\n\r\n1. Bind the exact handle from the actual execute response. Inspect actual flattened\r\n   columns. Schema-only responses aren't fact retrieval. If linkage is missing or\r\n   wrong, stop dependent use; don't obtain a plausible handle from memory.\r\n2. Prefer explicit typed columns, no Markdown, diagnostic prose or embedded arrays.\r\n   Read control/metrics, then checks, then Code, Source and conditional US summaries\r\n   in type-specific projections from the SAME retained result. Each contains the\r\n   actual scope, Country, Vendor, P/T, record type, dimension, ordinal and total;\r\n   keep scope-status and metric-version in every partition as well.\r\n   Control/metrics additionally need dates/calendar/version/status, selected count,\r\n   Country totals, Vendor PD/Open/ratios/contributions/rank/share/coverage. Checks\r\n   need required, counts-match, bucket counts, actual/expected P/T totals and signed\r\n   totals. Summary groups need eta-value,row-kind,display-rank,represented Other\r\n   count, bucket count, P/T PD,delta,relative-change,delta-country-contribution.\r\n3. Lossless type partitions and bounded page windows are permitted over `data`\r\n   ONLY. This is retained-result reading, not source SQL or a new fact request.\r\n   The disjoint partitions must collectively cover all returned record types/rows.\r\n   Any unexpected type must be read and reported, not discarded. Keep a coverage\r\n   ledger of raw identities/ordinals; per-group expected source count remains the\r\n   whole source count, not that group's count. Never compare one partition's length\r\n   to the whole result and call it incomplete without considering the union.\r\n4. Inspect BOTH row and cell truncation metadata on every read. `truncated?=false`\r\n   or result-complete=true alone is insufficient. Start bounded (e.g. ≤40 rows),\r\n   use actual supported paging to continue; don't assume a fixed preview cap.\r\n   Fewer columns won't fix a long string cell. Affected names remain display-limited;\r\n   affected identities/metrics remain unverified. Do not turn cut-off text into facts.\r\n5. Stored full values may still exist when a preview is truncated. Use only a\r\n   discovered supported full-value read/export if available. No invented substring,\r\n   length override, raw export or local script. If unavailable, report the precise\r\n   affected field/scope. No retries of giant Markdown blocks.\r\n\r\nIllustrative retained-only SQL, ONLY after discovering these actual column names\r\nand confirming reader support for WHERE/LIMIT/OFFSET. These are NOT ClickHouse\r\nqueries, NOT proven host syntax, and NOT a full report projection:\r\n\r\nControl+metrics partition (use the exact same handle for the following partitions):\r\n\r\n```sql\r\nSELECT \"scope_key\", \"scope_status\", \"metric_version\", \"record_type\", \"country_code\",\r\n       \"vendor_code\", \"vendor_name\", \"previous_snapshot_date\", \"target_snapshot_date\",\r\n       \"calendar_mode\", \"selection_mode\", \"business_timezone\", \"queried_at_local\",\r\n       \"selected_vendor_count\", \"contribution_rank\", \"previous_past_due_count\",\r\n       \"target_past_due_count\", \"past_due_count_change\", \"previous_open_count\",\r\n       \"target_open_count\", \"previous_vendor_ratio\", \"target_vendor_ratio\",\r\n       \"previous_country_open\", \"target_country_open\",\r\n       \"previous_country_past_due_count\", \"target_country_past_due_count\",\r\n       \"previous_country_ratio\", \"target_country_ratio\", \"previous_country_contribution\",\r\n       \"target_country_contribution\", \"delta_country_contribution\",\r\n       \"positive_contribution_share\", \"selected_positive_coverage\",\r\n       \"row_ordinal\", \"expected_record_count\"\r\nFROM data\r\nWHERE \"record_type\" IN ('COUNTRY_CONTROL', 'VENDOR_METRICS')\r\nORDER BY \"row_ordinal\" ASC\r\nLIMIT 40 OFFSET 0\r\n```\r\n\r\nCheck partition (don't replace required=false with a failed result):\r\n\r\n```sql\r\nSELECT \"scope_key\", \"scope_status\", \"metric_version\", \"record_type\", \"country_code\",\r\n       \"vendor_code\", \"previous_snapshot_date\", \"target_snapshot_date\", \"dimension\",\r\n       \"required\", \"counts_match\", \"expected_bucket_count\", \"previous_total\",\r\n       \"target_total\", \"previous_expected_total\", \"target_expected_total\",\r\n       \"increase_total\", \"decrease_total\", \"net_change\", \"row_ordinal\", \"expected_record_count\"\r\nFROM data\r\nWHERE \"record_type\" = 'VENDOR_CHECK'\r\nORDER BY \"row_ordinal\" ASC\r\nLIMIT 40 OFFSET 0\r\n```\r\n\r\nCode partition (repeat with ETA_SOURCE_SUMMARY and US_SOURCE_SUMMARY as separate\r\ndisjoint partitions; preserve columns/handle, read further supported pages as needed):\r\n\r\n```sql\r\nSELECT \"scope_key\", \"scope_status\", \"metric_version\", \"record_type\", \"country_code\", \"vendor_code\",\r\n       \"previous_snapshot_date\", \"target_snapshot_date\", \"dimension\",\r\n       \"eta_value\", \"row_kind\", \"display_rank\", \"other_bucket_count\",\r\n       \"expected_bucket_count\", \"previous_past_due_count\", \"target_past_due_count\",\r\n       \"past_due_count_change\", \"past_due_count_relative_change\",\r\n       \"delta_country_contribution\", \"row_ordinal\", \"expected_record_count\"\r\nFROM data\r\nWHERE \"record_type\" = 'ETA_CODE_SUMMARY'\r\nORDER BY \"row_ordinal\" ASC\r\nLIMIT 40 OFFSET 0\r\n```\r\n\r\nSource/ETA/rank predicates are NOT allowed here. WHERE may partition only declared\r\nrecord types or bounded stable row-ordinal windows, always with accounted union.\r\nNo metric expressions, aggregation, window functions, joins, other tables, writes,\r\nSELECT *, substring recovery guesses or source/denominator/rank recomputation.\r\nValues must retain original units. Quote actual discovered identifiers; don't\r\nassume underscores. Use native supported reader paging instead where available.\r\nIf partition/paging syntax isn't supported, retain the same data projection and\r\nuse discovered native paging; if no complete route exists, stop that scope as a\r\ntransport limitation rather than substituting a different analysis interface.\r\nThe five partitions above cover exactly the six declared report record types.\r\nThese SQL examples are reading shapes, not executed evidence. If a source response\r\ncontains another type, account for and inspect it before claiming complete coverage.\r\n\r\n## Binding, budgets and failures\r\n\r\nInterpret record-type before fields: VENDOR_METRICS holds Vendor totals; summary\r\nrows hold bucket counts. See data-contract.md binding table. Check dimension=us\r\nfor ANY Country, not only Country US; joint summaries never come from stitching\r\nmarginal Code/Source rows. Other stays a mixed remainder. A required=false zero\r\njoint check is not failed. Read all applicable checks before Country diagnosis.\r\n\r\nOne justified correction retry per logical request; pagination isn't a retry.\r\nChanging source limit/projection does not fix cancelled aggregation. Preserve\r\nindependent valid facts; nested errors count even if outer isError=false.\r\nINTERACTIVE: ≤6 different logical fact requests; discovery/result reads/paging\r\nexcluded. Presets have no six-request cap, but optional/history breadth is explicit.\r\nNo per-Vendor fallback storms. Complete rows/checks do not certify final text.\r\n\r\nCapture labels use returned America/Los_Angeles; actual PST/PDT+offset only with\r\ntrusted instant evidence. Date pinning is not a transaction. Compare only common\r\nsame-scope semantic fields, not projection shape or capture timestamps. Error\r\nclassification and delivery checks live in completion-contract.md.\r\n"
  }

  {
    "type": "text",
    "text": "# Atlas data contract — structured default 3.3.0\r\n\r\nConfirm the live MCP schema before execution; these contracts do not prove a\r\nparticular environment is deployed. The development source is\r\n`scripts/build_v31_contract.py` (REPORT_FIELDS, EXPLORE_INPUTS, EXPLORE_FIELDS).\r\nIt is not an Agent runtime dependency. Long-text block collections are retained compatibility-only. Inventory counts are not online acceptance.\r\nRequired new interfaces missing → deployment gap, never silent v3/v2 fallback.\r\n\r\n## Flexible analysis: pastdue.explore-query/rows\r\n\r\nAll 20 entity inputs are explicit:\r\n\r\n| Input | Values / semantics |\r\n|---|---|\r\n| country-code | Authorized Country; no implicit cross-Country union |\r\n| time-basis | CURRENT / SNAPSHOT / PAIR / RANGE |\r\n| target-snapshot-date | Exact completed eligible target for SNAPSHOT/PAIR |\r\n| previous-snapshot-date | Exact previous for PAIR+EXPLICIT; PREVIOUS_AVAILABLE resolves it server-side |\r\n| range-start-date, range-end-date | Inclusive target range for RANGE; 1..366 calendar dates, end before local today |\r\n| calendar-mode | WEEKDAYS / CALENDAR_DAYS; default WEEKDAYS, explicit weekend override |\r\n| pair-policy | PREVIOUS_AVAILABLE / EXPLICIT; exact explicit pair must never be substituted |\r\n| grain | COUNTRY / VENDOR / VENDOR_CODE / VENDOR_SOURCE / VENDOR_CODE_SOURCE |\r\n| vendor-code | Numeric Vendor; 0 = all, never a real Vendor row |\r\n| eta-code-filter | Empty = none; trim-uppercase exact code, UNKNOWN for empty code |\r\n| source-filter | Empty = none; trimmed exact raw source, case preserved; UNKNOWN for empty source |\r\n| department-type-filter | Empty = none; trimmed case-insensitive exact mapping value |\r\n| master-primary-filter, master-manager-filter | Empty = none; trimmed exact current mapping values |\r\n| denominator-scope | COUNTRY_ALL / FILTERED_POPULATION / WITHIN_VENDOR |\r\n| ranking-metric | NONE / PAST_DUE_COUNT / PAST_DUE_RATIO / PAST_DUE_SHARE / COUNT_CHANGE / RATIO_CHANGE / CONTRIBUTION_CHANGE |\r\n| sort-direction | ASC / DESC |\r\n| change-selection | ALL / POSITIVE / NEGATIVE / ZERO |\r\n| top-n | 0 = all or integer 1..100; applies to grouped FACT rows, not automatically distinct Vendors |\r\n\r\nSupply inactive date inputs as `1970-01-01` typed sentinels; they are NOT observed\r\nsnapshot dates. CURRENT returns today's local date as a capture label, not a\r\ncompleted snapshot. Non-PAIR previous fields are placeholders and must not be\r\nrendered as actual previous facts or meaningful changes. RANGE returns date-grain\r\nstocks, not period sums; top-n is global over result rows, not per date.\r\n\r\nChange ranking or non-ALL change-selection requires PAIR. WITHIN_VENDOR is invalid\r\nat COUNTRY grain. With RATIO_CHANGE sign uses ratio delta; with\r\nCONTRIBUTION_CHANGE sign uses contribution delta; otherwise sign uses count delta.\r\nNONE uses deterministic date/Vendor/Code/Source ordering. Rank ties use target date,\r\nVendor, Code, Source; null rank metrics sort last. Null is not zero.\r\n\r\nRows use **pastdue.explore-fact**. Always project scope-key. One `record-type=CONTROL` plus `FACT` rows only when scope-status=OK:\r\n- Control: scope-status, expected-record-count, row-ordinal and request descriptors.\r\n  Statuses: OK / NO_DATA / DATA_ERROR / INVALID_SELECTION /\r\n  UNSUPPORTED_COMBINATION / INVALID_DATE / INVALID_PAIR / INVALID_RANGE.\r\n- Identity/descriptors: country-code, vendor-code, vendor-name,\r\n  target-snapshot-date, previous-snapshot-date, calendar-mode, time-basis, grain,\r\n  eta-code, eta-source, denominator-scope, metric-version, organization-basis,\r\n  business-timezone, queried-at-local. scope-key encodes ALL required inputs, including filters/pair-policy. Retain\r\n  original inputs with provenance; individual filter attributes are not echoed.\r\n- Counts: target/previous-past-due-count, target/previous-open-count,\r\n  target/previous-ratio-denominator, target/previous-share-denominator,\r\n  target/previous-country-open, past-due-count-change, selection-rank,\r\n  expected-record-count, row-ordinal.\r\n- Decimals: target/previous-past-due-ratio, target/previous-past-due-share,\r\n  past-due-count-relative-change, past-due-ratio-change, delta-country-contribution.\r\n\r\nRequire exactly one OK control before using FACT rows. Control OK with no FACT\r\nrows is a verified empty selection only after complete pagination and expected\r\ncount validation; NO_DATA is not a normal business finding. Sparse control fields\r\nthat don't apply to its record-type are placeholders, not measurements.\r\n\r\nDenominators are independent of ETA row filters. COUNTRY_ALL share denominator\r\nis whole Country past due; FILTERED_POPULATION is Vendor+org-filtered population\r\npast due before ETA filters; WITHIN_VENDOR is each scoped Vendor's past due before\r\nETA filters. Ratio denominator is whole Country Open at COUNTRY grain and whole\r\nVendor Open at Vendor grains, not bucket Open, not share denominator and not\r\nchanged by denominator-scope. `*-open-count` is grouped row Open, not necessarily\r\n`*-ratio-denominator`. Org filters use deduplicated CURRENT_MAPPING even for\r\nhistorical data; disclose that it is not historical ownership.\r\n\r\n## Standard structured report: pastdue.report-query/rows\r\n\r\nSame seven inputs as `pastdue.analysis-query`:\r\ncountry-code, target-snapshot-date, previous-snapshot-date, calendar-mode,\r\nselection-mode, top-n, vendor-code. POSITIVE_TOP_N: top-n=1..10, vendor-code=0;\r\nSINGLE_VENDOR: actual Vendor, top-n=1, includes zero/negative contributions.\r\nUnlike explore EXPLICIT, the report pair must be the nearest earlier available\r\neligible snapshot for the target; use explore for arbitrary earlier explicit pairs.\r\n\r\nAll rows use **pastdue.report-fact**, discriminated by `record-type`:\r\n\r\n| record-type | Meaning / fields that matter |\r\n|---|---|\r\n| COUNTRY_CONTROL | Exactly one scope-status, selected-vendor-count, target/previous-country-open, target/previous-country-past-due-count, target/previous-country-ratio |\r\n| VENDOR_METRICS | One per selected Vendor: contribution-rank, vendor-name, target/previous-past-due-count, past-due-count-change, target/previous-open-count, target/previous-vendor-ratio, target/previous-country-contribution, delta-country-contribution, positive-contribution-share, selected-positive-coverage |\r\n| ETA_CODE_SUMMARY | dimension=code; compact full signed Code summary |\r\n| ETA_SOURCE_SUMMARY | dimension=source; compact full signed Source summary |\r\n| US_SOURCE_SUMMARY | dimension=us; Source buckets conditioned on Code=US, not marginal Source |\r\n| VENDOR_CHECK | One per selected Vendor × code/source/us, including empty/nonrequired dimensions |\r\n\r\nCommon projected fields: scope-key, record-type, country-code, vendor-code,\r\ntarget-snapshot-date, previous-snapshot-date, calendar-mode, selection-mode,\r\nmetric-version, scope-status, row-ordinal, expected-record-count,\r\nbusiness-timezone, queried-at-local. Check COUNTRY_CONTROL scope-status=OK before\r\nclaiming selection validity. Analysis scope errors remain INVALID_PAIR /\r\nINVALID_SELECTION / INVALID_DENOMINATOR / DATA_ERROR / VENDOR_NOT_FOUND.\r\n\r\nSummary fields: dimension, eta-value, row-kind, display-rank,\r\nexpected-bucket-count, other-bucket-count, previous/target-past-due-count,\r\npast-due-count-change, past-due-count-relative-change,\r\ndelta-country-contribution, increase-total, decrease-total, net-change,\r\nprevious-total, target-total, counts-match, required.\r\n`row-kind=BUCKET`, display-rank=1..5 are actual buckets; `row-kind=OTHER`, rank=6\r\nis the server aggregate remainder, with blank eta-value. Render it as Other/其他;\r\nnever as UNKNOWN, never as an extra raw bucket. Other exists only for a remainder.\r\n\r\nVENDOR_CHECK fields: dimension, required, counts-match, expected-bucket-count,\r\nprevious-total, target-total, previous-expected-total, target-expected-total,\r\nincrease-total, decrease-total, net-change. Code/Source expected totals are Vendor\r\nPD; US expected totals are Code=US PD. Required=false is explicit nonapplicability,\r\nnot a missing check. Do not read sparse defaults on unrelated record types as facts.\r\n\r\nReport3.3 pagination default/max=1000 (other collection defaults unchanged), so the\r\ncompact Top10 bound221 fits one source page if actual Atlas honors config. This\r\ndoes not guarantee complete retained-result preview reads. Every report row has expected-record-count (INCLUDING COUNTRY_CONTROL) and a\r\nunique row-ordinal. expected-bucket-count is RAW normalized bucket count, not\r\nnumber of compact summary records. Validate BUCKET count + sum(OTHER\r\nother-bucket-count) against it. The server computes all full-population metrics,\r\nTop-5, Other and signed totals from shared facts. The Agent verifies, not rebuilds.\r\n\r\n## Monitor, history and compatibility\r\n\r\n`pastdue.monitor-query/countries`: calendar-mode, relative-change-threshold=0.10;\r\n`pastdue.monitor-fact` contains actual pair, Country PD/Open/ratios, business/data\r\nstatus, expected-target-date, target-lag-workdays, missing-workdays-between and gap.\r\n`pastdue.history-query/comparisons` or `/trigger-events`: country-code,\r\ncalendar-mode, range-start-date, range-end-date, relative-change-threshold;\r\n`pastdue.history-fact` gives eligible target comparisons/events. Previous may\r\nprecede range start. Every requested Country must be represented in the report.\r\n\r\nLegacy analysis/current-analysis and contact/Owner/detail/mapping interfaces stay\r\ncompatible, but are not silent replacements for explore/report. Optional current\r\ncontext remains on demand. No local graph-file reads at Agent runtime. Absent\r\nlive version/watermark is UNVERIFIED, never an invented transaction guarantee.\r\n\r\n## Scope and capture provenance\r\nBoth fact namespaces include mandatory scope-key (string), the SQL JSON tuple\r\nof ALL required inputs in declaration order, incorporated into row identity.\r\nAlways project and retain it; compare exact keys within the captured request/pages.\r\nIt preserves complete filter/selection/ranking scope and avoids identity collision;\r\nit is NOT a source revision, watermark, immutable-snapshot or transaction token.\r\nCountry control PD/Open/ratios must reconcile to pinned monitor/history T/P facts\r\nbefore combining captures. Drift is disclosed and affected comparisons unverified,\r\nnot silently reconciled by overwriting counts. Paired raw provenance is needed to\r\nclassify a hard same-scope DATA_CONFLICT rather than changing capture state.\r\nqueried-at-local is an actual capture timestamp and business-timezone is IANA;\r\ntrusted runtime supplies the instant's actual PST/PDT and UTC offset. If a local\r\nfall-back timestamp is ambiguous and no trusted instant/offset exists, disclose\r\nthe ambiguity rather than choosing an offset. No implied cross-query transaction.\r\n\r\n### Capture and semantic-comparison safeguard\r\nShow available queried-at-local and business-timezone; derive actual PST/PDT offset\r\nonly from its trusted corresponding instant/offset, never a guessed tool-capture or\r\ndelivery time. Without that evidence retain the returned local timestamp/IANA and\r\nlabel offset UNVERIFIED. Legacy queried-at-utc keeps UTC semantics unchanged; the\r\nnew queried-at-local field never repurposes an old UTC attribute. DATA_CONFLICT\r\ncompares COMMON semantic fact fields in paired raw same-scope responses, not whole\r\npayload shape: projection-only fields, handles or timestamps differing aren't a\r\nconflict, and an unprojected field is unknown, not contradictory.\r\n\r\n## Governed retained-result reading\r\n\r\nSee atlas-query-playbook.md for the single authoritative reading procedure.\r\nSource pagination and retained-result pagination are distinct. Explicit-column\r\nreads, supported bounded paging and lossless record-type partitions of the exact\r\nretained `data` result are allowed. No source SQL, joins, recomputed metrics or\r\nsilently discarded rows. Check cell truncation as well as rows. Materialization\r\nis not another logical fact request. Long Markdown/JSON fields are not required.\r\n\r\n## Report row binding\r\n\r\nThe flat `pastdue.report-fact` schema is a tagged union, NOT one interchangeable\r\nmetric row. Identical field names have different meanings by record-type. Before\r\nrendering, partition returned rows by the following selectors; keep their actual\r\nhandle and row-ordinal as provenance. This is an Agent procedure, not a new runtime\r\nservice, required tool or claim of machine enforcement.\r\n\r\n| Consumer | Required selector | Bound fields / meaning |\r\n|---|---|---|\r\n| Country cross-check | COUNTRY_CONTROL | Country PD/Open/ratio, selected-vendor-count, expected-record-count |\r\n| Vendor overview AND detailed metrics | VENDOR_METRICS + exact Country/Vendor/scope | previous/target-past-due-count, previous/target-open-count, previous/target-vendor-ratio, contribution-rank, previous/target-country-contribution, delta-country-contribution, positive-contribution-share |\r\n| Code table | ETA_CODE_SUMMARY + dimension=code + same Vendor/scope | bucket counts, count change, relative change, delta-country-contribution, row-kind, display-rank |\r\n| Source table | ETA_SOURCE_SUMMARY + dimension=source + same Vendor/scope | independent marginal Source bucket fields |\r\n| Internal conditional-source evidence (optional detail on request) | US_SOURCE_SUMMARY + dimension=us + same Vendor/scope | Source counts conditioned on Code=US, ANY Country |\r\n| Signed totals and applicability | VENDOR_CHECK + same Vendor/scope + exact dimension | required, counts-match, previous/target-total, previous/target-expected-total, increase-total, decrease-total, net-change, expected-bucket-count |\r\n| Selected coverage | agreeing VENDOR_METRICS metadata in same selection | selected-positive-coverage, NOT sum of this repeated field |\r\n\r\nScope includes scope-key, Country, actual P/T dates, calendar, selection mode and\r\nmetric version, with exposed environment provenance retained. Maintain one bound\r\nVendor entry per selected VENDOR_METRICS row, in returned contribution-rank order.\r\nAttach code/source/us summaries and checks by identity, never adjacency or totals.\r\nDuplicate/missing metrics or check rows are errors, not “use first/last”. A summary\r\nfailure must not overwrite Vendor metrics or unrelated dimensions.\r\n\r\n`target-country-contribution` is a level, NOT a change. Only\r\n`delta-country-contribution` fills contribution-change cells. The value is a ratio;\r\npercentage-point formatting multiplies by 100 once. Vendor ratio uses Vendor Open;\r\nCountry contribution uses Country Open. Do not fill one from another field.\r\nVendor totals cannot be read from a SH or US bucket just because those rows share\r\nprevious/target-past-due-count columns. Sparse defaults on unrelated types are\r\nnever fallback values. Render overview and detail from the same bound metrics.\r\n\r\nCode and Source must each independently reconcile to both Vendor PD totals. US\r\nmust reconcile to Code=US (possibly hidden in Code Other), not all Source. Use\r\nper-Vendor dimension=us check in CA as well as US. Positive visible Code=US with\r\nrequired=false, or differing joint totals, is a reconciliation failure affecting\r\nUS joint interpretation; do not call it not applicable or erase valid Code/Source.\r\nIf raw Code=US is hidden in Other, verified server checks are the evidence boundary,\r\nnot proof of an independent raw-bucket audit. No marginal-distribution stitching.\r\n\r\nUse independent arithmetic only as a consistency check on returned counts/rates,\r\nnever to silently replace a missing or inconsistent authoritative field. A failed\r\ncomparison is explicitly UNVERIFIED/failed at the narrowest affected scope. Keep\r\nraw rows and request references; numeric disagreement inside a single response\r\nis a reconciliation failure, not proof of two-response DATA_CONFLICT.\r\n\r\n\r\nAgent display contract 3.3.0 hides the dedicated conditional-source table in\r\nstandard reports; the facts/checks above remain unchanged. Material business\r\ninterpretation is integrated into Country Vendor analysis immediately after Vendor\r\nranking, with exact Vendor identity retained. This is not a new Country-wide\r\naggregate or permission to infer associations from independent distributions.\r\n"
  }
]

[
  {
    "type": "text",
    "text": "# Report modules — 3.3.0 (single layout authority)\r\n\r\nUse structured facts, not 3.2 long Markdown blocks. Same modules and column order\r\nin Chinese and English; choose ONE language. Code/Vendor names remain literal data.\r\nInteractive answers compose only relevant modules. Standard reports use all\r\napplicable modules below; explicit brief/custom output discloses its reduced scope.\r\n\r\n## Shared formatting\r\n\r\nDate YYYY-MM-DD, pairs previous→target, integers without thousands separators,\r\npositive changes +, negative changes −, zero 0. Ratios/shares/relative changes:\r\n100×returned ratio, exactly two decimals %. Contribution levels/changes: 100×ratio,\r\nexactly four decimals pp. Round for display only; don't recompute formulas.\r\nIf a nonzero change rounds to zero, use '<0.01%' / '<0.0001 pp' with its actual\r\ndirection in words rather than a misleading signed zero. Exact zero remains 0.\r\nEscape table delimiters/newlines in names. Do not change raw identity to fit display.\r\nNull relative change with P=0,T>0: 零基线上升 / Zero-baseline increase; P=T=0:\r\n无变化 / No change. Missing, failed and unqueried are not zero or the same '—'.\r\nUse 无数据/No data only for verified successful empty results; 未查询/Not queried,\r\n查询失败/Query failed, 未验证完整/Completeness unverified describe their actual cases.\r\n\r\n## Standard section order\r\n\r\n1. **运行信息与结论 / Run information and conclusion**: DAILY/HISTORICAL/TEST,\r\n   resolved language, America/Los_Angeles, actual P/T per Country. Show actual\r\n   PST/PDT+offset only with trusted instant evidence. TEST title [TEST]/[测试],\r\n   preserve actual trigger and show TEST_FORCED/THRESHOLD_MET separately.\r\n2. **Country 指标 / Country metrics**: all requested Countries, default US→CA.\r\n3. For each applicable **{Country} — {P}→{T}**:\r\n   - **Vendor 排名 / Vendor ranking**\r\n   - **Country Vendor 总结分析 / Country Vendor analysis summary**\r\n   - **#{rank} {Vendor Code} — {name}**, EVERY selected Vendor in the same order.\r\n   - **覆盖率与限制 / Coverage and limitations**\r\n4. **跨 Country 总结 / Cross-Country summary** (multiple Countries only).\r\n5. **执行范围与缺失项 / Execution scope and gaps**: compact actual source checks,\r\n   rows retrieved vs expected, Vendors delivered vs selected, exact missing scope.\r\n\r\nNo-trigger Countries still appear in metrics; ordinary drilldown is not required.\r\nValid zero-positive selection shows explicit empty selection, not fabricated rows.\r\nHistory events-only stops at event listing. No current monitor mislabeled historical.\r\n\r\n## Country metrics columns (exact order)\r\n\r\n| 中文 | English | Binding |\r\n|---|---|---|\r\n| Country | Country | country-code |\r\n| 业务状态 | Business status | business-status |\r\n| 数据状态 | Data status | data-status |\r\n| 上期日期 | Previous date | previous-snapshot-date |\r\n| 本期日期 | Target date | target-snapshot-date |\r\n| 间隔天数 | Gap days | snapshot-gap-days |\r\n| 逾期 上期→本期 | Past due previous→target | previous/target-past-due-count |\r\n| Open 上期→本期 | Open previous→target | previous/target-open-count |\r\n| 逾期率 上期→本期 | Ratio previous→target | previous/target-past-due-ratio |\r\n| 相对变化 | Relative change | relative-ratio-change |\r\n| 百分点变化 | Point change | percentage-point-change (ratio→pp) |\r\n\r\nDates not adjacent: say 较上一可用快照 / Compared with previous available snapshot,\r\nnot 'yesterday'; include calendar gap and delay/missing-weekday warnings when returned.\r\n\r\n## Vendor ranking columns (VENDOR_METRICS only)\r\n\r\n| 中文 | English | Binding |\r\n|---|---|---|\r\n| 排名 | Rank | contribution-rank |\r\n| Vendor Code／名称 | Vendor code / name | vendor-code + vendor-name |\r\n| 逾期 上期→本期 | Past due previous→target | previous/target-past-due-count |\r\n| 净变化 | Net change | past-due-count-change |\r\n| Open 上期→本期 | Open previous→target | previous/target-open-count |\r\n| Vendor 逾期率 上期→本期 | Vendor ratio previous→target | previous/target-vendor-ratio |\r\n| Country 贡献变化 pp | Country contribution change pp | delta-country-contribution |\r\n| 正向贡献占比 | Positive contribution share | positive-contribution-share |\r\n| 分析状态 | Analysis status | actual scoped checks/retrieval, not invented raw field |\r\n\r\nBind overview and details to the SAME metrics row. Never read Vendor totals from\r\nETA bucket rows. SINGLE_VENDOR retains actual rank and can have nonpositive delta;\r\npositive-share is not applicable there if its contribution is nonpositive.\r\n\r\n## Country Vendor analysis summary (immediately after ranking)\r\n\r\nWrite concise grounded interpretation: leading selected contributors; material\r\nincreases and improvement offsets; verified conditional Code=US Source meaning;\r\npriority checks; scope limits. Use rule IDs/meanings from eta-diagnostic-rules.edn.\r\nPreserve Vendor identity. Don't invent Country-wide aggregates from Top10 or add\r\nCode and Source. No standalone US joint-source column/table, no generic tutorial\r\ninstead of actual observed evidence. If no applicable joint activity, omit that\r\ninterpretation; if needed evidence failed, disclose only the affected limitation.\r\nUnknown/Other Source has no guessed classification. Compact Top5+Other supports\r\nonly its visible buckets and returned signed totals, not unseen constituents.\r\n\r\n## Every Vendor detail (fixed order)\r\n\r\n1. **指标变化 / Metric changes**: PD/Open/Vendor ratio P→T from ranking row;\r\n   Country contribution P→T and delta from that same row. One compact line/list,\r\n   no second wide metrics table.\r\n2. **ETA Code 变化 / ETA Code changes** table.\r\n3. **ETA Source 变化 / ETA Source changes** table.\r\n4. **变化摘要与诊断 / Change summary and diagnosis**: Code and Source EACH have\r\n   increase-total, decrease-total (negative signed), net-change from VENDOR_CHECK;\r\n   identify material worsening/improving categories, residual risk and suggested\r\n   check. Distinguish count change vs denominator effects. No PO migration/causality.\r\n\r\nBoth ETA tables use exactly these columns in this order:\r\n\r\n| ETA 值 / ETA value | 上期数量 / Previous count | 本期数量 / Target count | 净变化 / Net change | 相对变化 / Relative change | Country 贡献变化 pp / Country contribution change pp |\r\n|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\r\n\r\nChoose localized headers, not bilingual duplication. BUCKET order=display-rank;\r\nOTHER is 其他（N项）/Other (N items), server-computed counts and relative change.\r\nDo not add a zero Other or re-rank summary rows. A failed/unread dimension retains\r\nits heading and concrete failure/empty status, never a fabricated table.\r\n\r\n## Coverage, context and length\r\n\r\nCoverage = selected-positive-coverage once, not sum of repeated metadata:\r\nTop10覆盖全部正向贡献的X% / Top10 covers X% of all positive contributions.\r\nNever describe it as the explained fraction of net Country increase. Mention\r\nnegative offsets and limit conclusions to selected Vendors.\r\n\r\nOwner/contacts/PO remain on request, CURRENT and display-only; standard report\r\nneedn't repeat 'not queried' under every Vendor. If requested, show actual result\r\nor status under that Vendor. PO quotas remain unchanged.\r\n\r\nKeep all selected Vendor units. Save space by removing repeated definitions/status\r\nmatrices/raw JSON, not mandatory ETA facts. If evidenced output limits require\r\nparts, number them by Country/Vendor range and state delivered/remaining scope;\r\nfull report is not delivered until all parts are actually present. Do not promise\r\nunsupported automatic continuation. No model-issued overall report/test PASS.\r\n"
  }
]

[
  {
    "type": "text",
    "text": "# Evidence and completion — 3.3.0\r\n\r\nThis is an Agent procedure, not an installed runtime gate. Query execution,\r\nretrieval and delivery are separate; none proves independent final-output accuracy.\r\n\r\n## A. Source and identity\r\n\r\nRetain actual input, execute response, handle, exposed environment/version and\r\ncapture metadata. A handle must originate in that request. Missing linkage is\r\nUNVERIFIED; a proven wrong attachment is ASSOCIATION_ERROR. Do not repair it by\r\nmatching plausible numbers. Start with serial Country requests.\r\n\r\nRequire scope-status=OK before interpreting selection. Compare COUNTRY_CONTROL\r\nPD/Open/ratios to the pinned monitor/history. Dates/keys do not prove cross-query\r\ntransactions. Different captures that disagree remain unverified pending evidence;\r\nnever silently overwrite one with another. Counts exact; ratios use metric tolerance.\r\n\r\n## B. Retrieval\r\n\r\nUse the playbook's two-layer reading procedure. Track separately:\r\n- source expected records and actual unique rows from all EQL pages;\r\n- retained-result rows actually read, across all reading partitions/pages;\r\n- cell truncation affecting each field, not only top-level truncated?.\r\n\r\nExactly expected rows with cut-off cells is not complete retrieval. A successful\r\nSELECT over an incomplete retained result is not source completeness. A truncated\r\nname limits name display, not independently intact numeric facts under verified\r\nCountry/Vendor identity. A truncated identity/scope field requires a supported\r\nfull-value read or leaves the affected association unverified. Don't silently\r\nstrip an ellipsis or assume it was part of the original value.\r\n\r\n## C. Server reconciliation and compact representation\r\n\r\n- Exactly one COUNTRY_CONTROL, including valid empty positive selection.\r\n- Exactly selected-vendor-count unique VENDOR_METRICS, with stable returned ranks.\r\n- Exactly one VENDOR_CHECK per selected Vendor×code/source/us, including empty paths.\r\n- Code and Source each match Vendor two-period PD. Joint us matches Code=US PD.\r\n- required=false with zero expected joint totals is NOT_APPLICABLE, not failure.\r\n  Nonzero US expected/visible counts with required=false is inconsistent.\r\n- Required checks need counts-match=true and matching expected/actual totals.\r\n- Each summary BUCKET has unique identity/display-rank; OTHER is one server\r\n  remainder, not UNKNOWN. BUCKET rows plus Other represented bucket counts must\r\n  match expected-bucket-count. Empty nonzero-total dimensions are not valid empties.\r\n- Never add Code and Source together. Read signed totals from VENDOR_CHECK;\r\n  Other net delta does not expose its internal increase/decrease composition.\r\n\r\nThese support server full-population reconciliation and compact-summary coverage,\r\nNOT client independent raw-bucket audit. Generic source-completeness UNKNOWN alone\r\nneither proves failure nor overrides valid identities/counts/checks. Record the\r\nactual evidence and any remaining source limitation.\r\n\r\n## D. Delivery\r\n\r\nBefore sending, compare Vendor overview set/order to detail set/order; verify each\r\nhas metrics, both ETA modules, signed totals and explanation or local failure.\r\nCheck language, dates, signs and delta-vs-level field binding against original rows.\r\nThis self-review is not independent validation and cannot guarantee final accuracy.\r\n\r\nReport the actual analysis scope as complete/partial/unavailable/not required.\r\nKeep actual business trigger and freshness separate. A source/read success with\r\nmissing Vendor sections is an incomplete delivery. Confirmed wrong rendered facts\r\nare REPORT_ERROR; correct the output, not the valid source. No model-issued overall\r\nPASS, invented evaluation score or source-hash proof of final text correctness.\r\nIf independent validation is asked about, say 'not performed' unless real evidence\r\nexists; do not burden every successful Vendor with that technical disclaimer.\r\n\r\nTEST shows forced vs real trigger, Countries/Vendors exercised, required steps\r\nexecuted and missing/unexercised scope. Zero-positive selection can complete its\r\napplicable empty path but did not exercise multi-Vendor ETA. No sending/escalation.\r\n\r\n## Local failures and retry\r\n\r\nA failed Code query/check does not erase Source or metrics. Failed joint evidence\r\nlimits its diagnostic, not ordinary Source display. Never substitute zero for\r\nquery failure or old positive-only drivers for signed changes.\r\n\r\nDATA_CONFLICT requires TWO contradictory original responses under the same full\r\nsemantic identity and common field. Model drafts are not evidence. Projection,\r\nformat, time/version/scope differences alone are not same-fact contradictions.\r\nSingle-response mismatch is reconciliation failure, not paired conflict.\r\n\r\nOne evidence-based correction retry per logical request. Unsupported result-read\r\nsyntax may be corrected after discovering actual support. Cancellation alone does\r\nnot identify its initiator; reducing projection/limit doesn't fix source aggregation.\r\nNo permissions bypass, per-Vendor retry storm or fabricated platform root cause.\r\n"
  }
]

\"MISSING_VENDOR_ACK\"\r\n     :requires-joint-facts false} {:code \"US\"\r\n     :source-condition \"AUTOUS\"\r\n     :meaning {:zh \"Vendor 提供的 ETA 已 past due\"\r\n       :en \"The Vendor-provided ETA is past due\"}\r\n     :verification {:zh \"核查原 ETA 有效性与下一次更新\"\r\n       :en \"Verify the original ETA and obtain the next update\"}\r\n     :rule-id \"ETA-US-AUTOUS-OVERDUE\"\r\n     :risk-type \"VENDOR_ETA_OVERDUE\"\r\n     :requires-joint-facts true} {:code \"US\"\r\n     :source-condition \"OTHER_KNOWN\"\r\n     :meaning {:zh \"Vendor 仅提供粗略时间 rough date\"\r\n       :en \"The Vendor has provided only a rough date\"}\r\n     :verification {:zh \"核查更明确的交期\"\r\n       :en \"Request a more precise delivery date\"}\r\n     :rule-id \"ETA-US-KNOWN-SOURCE-ROUGH-DATE\"\r\n     :risk-type \"ROUGH_DATE_ONLY\"\r\n     :requires-joint-facts true} {:code \"US\"\r\n     :source-condition \"UNKNOWN\"\r\n     :meaning {:zh \"来源未知，无法区分具体业务含义\"\r\n       :en \"Source is unknown; the specific US interpretation cannot be determined\"}\r\n     :verification {:zh \"先确认来源，不按其他来源解释\"\r\n       :en \"Identify the source first; do not treat it as a known non-AutoUS source\"}\r\n     :rule-id \"ETA-US-UNKNOWN-SOURCE\"\r\n     :risk-type \"SOURCE_UNDETERMINED\"\r\n     :requires-joint-facts true}]\r\n  :source-classification {:authority \"governed business Source dictionary; accepted observed values below, not a new data table\"\r\n    :recognized-other-sources [\"AUTO NR\" \"API ASN\" \"API Order\" \"BUYER\" \"Block AUTOETA\" \"Carrier API\" \"EDI855\" \"EDI856\" \"EDI870\" \"EDI3B2\" \"EDI3A4C\" \"EDI3A6\" \"Vendor Email\" \"Vendor Report\" \"Vendor Web Site\" \"Vendor Invoice\" \"AUTOBUY\" \"PO_REL\" \"Auto Push\" \"NIFI_AUTO_REC\"]\r\n    :match \"trim-case-insensitive-exact; no substring, prefix or speculative aliases\"\r\n    :requires-observed-source-in-verified-joint-row true\r\n    :precedence [\"AUTOUS canonical or explicit alias\" \"OTHER_KNOWN only recognized dictionary member\" \"UNKNOWN for empty, UNKNOWN literal or any unrecognized raw value\"]\r\n    :unrecognized-source-action \"retain raw Source; diagnostic class UNKNOWN; no rough-date classification\"\r\n    :catalog-expansion \"only explicit governed Source dictionary approval; no invented lookup interface or table\"\r\n    :dictionary-membership-not-evidence-of-presence true\r\n    :source-name-does-not-add-business-semantics true\r\n    :other-summary-action \"mixed aggregate; no source-specific rule without constituent joint facts\"}\r\n  :application {:verified-identity-and-dimension-required true\r\n    :zero-zero \"NO_SIGNAL\"\r\n    :previous-zero-target-positive \"ZERO_BASELINE_INCREASE\"\r\n    :positive-target-decreasing \"IMPROVING_WITH_RESIDUAL_RISK\"\r\n    :positive-to-zero \"CATEGORY_CLEARED_NOT_PO_CAUSE_PROOF\"\r\n    :positive-unchanged \"PERSISTING_NOT_WORSENING\"\r\n    :increasing \"WORSENING\"\r\n    :quantity-down-contribution-up \"explain Country denominator effect, not count worsening\"\r\n    :other \"mixed aggregate; no inferred constituents/rule classification\"\r\n    :unknown \"retain raw value; no invented business meaning\"\r\n    :summary-scope \"selected Vendors only, never whole Country causality\"}}\r\n"
  }

g1
{

---

**Cursor**

越改越差，是因为 3.3.2 / 3.3.3 把一次本来能过的调用，写成了这个宿主做不到的形状。3.3.0 能跑完，不是因为它更严格，而是它让模型少填字段，直接读行。

## 成功版实际提交了什么

3.3.0 的 `analyze_result` 只有有值的键，例如：

```json
{
  "result_handle": "result_a7989131-a408-4f7b-86f7-33270aa52997",
  "sql": "SELECT * FROM data ORDER BY country_code",
  "analysis_version": 2
}
```

报告读取同样是 `result_handle` + 非空 `sql` + `analysis_version: 2`。没有 `query`，没有 `result_handles`，也没有先打一轮“只传 handle”的 discovery。服务端接受了，US 177 行、CA 154 行都读到了。

## 失败版被改成了什么

3.3.3 要求先只传：

```json
{"result_handle":"<本次 execute 的 handle>"}
```

并且反复写明不要带 `query`、`result_handles`、空 `sql`。这个宿主上的模型读完这段说明后，反而把工具表单填满：

```json
{
  "query": "",
  "result_handle": "result_75c83a47-...",
  "result_handles": [],
  "sql": "",
  "analysis_version": 1,
  "infer_time": false,
  "input_row_limit": 20000
}
```

空字符串和空数组仍算“提交了选择器”，所以报 `give exactly one of ...`。第二次原样再打，然后按新规则停掉。调用还没到 SQL，日报就结束了。

也就是说：后加的参数说明没有改变服务端，只改变了模型的出站习惯，而且改到了这个平台会拒绝的方向。

## 还有一条规则和成功日志相反

成功日志里，监控 `execute_eql` 界面上的 handle 是旧的 `result_5387f9d4...`（`captured-at` 仍是更早那次），真正读出行的是另一个 handle，日期是 2026-10-27 → 2026-10-28。CA 的 `execute` 界面甚至重复显示了 US 的 handle，随后用 `result_352cf459...` 才读出 CA 的 154 行。

3.3.0 在这种对不上的情况下继续读，读到了新数据。3.3.3 规定 handle 必须与界面上的 `execute` 返回值一致，不一致就停。按这条规则，成功那次也会被判失败。

## 该怎么收

线上继续用 **3.3.0 的调用方式**，不要再部署 3.3.2 / 3.3.3 那套“先 discovery、省略键、handle 必须逐字相等”的说明。

保留下来、并且这次已经验证过的读法是：

- `execute_eql` 仍带非空 `query` 和 `retain_result: true`
- `analyze_result` 只带本次要用的 `result_handle`、非空 `sql`、`analysis_version: 2`
- 监控可以直接 `SELECT * FROM data`
- 报告继续按 `record_type` 分段查询，不要先打一轮空参数 discovery

3.3.0 这份结果里还有一个业务缺口，不是后来改坏的：Country Control 的 Country 总量是 0，和监控里的 US 39330→38281、CA 6253→5833 对不上。Vendor 行本身是读到了，只是不能把 Country 总量当成已经核对通过。

- 客户端不要序列化未填写的参数，不要把字符串初始化成 `""`、把数组初始化成 `[]`。
- 或者服务端在做「三者只能有一个」判断之前，把空白的 `query`、空白的 `result_handle`、空的 `result_handles` 视为未提供。

15:54 那次成功的 `analyze_result` 实际只有这三项：

```json
{
  "result_handle": "result_a7989131-a408-4f7b-86f7-33270aa52997",
  "sql": "SELECT * FROM data ORDER BY country_code",
  "analysis_version": 2
}
```

请求里没有 `query`，也没有 `result_handles`。服务端的规则是这三者只能出现一个，所以它接受了，US 177 行、CA 154 行都读到了。

16:12 失败的请求是同一条规则，只是请求体变成了七项：`query` 是空字符串，`result_handles` 是空数组，再加上句柄。空字符串和空数组都算“提交了这个选择器”，于是被拒绝。SQL 和 `analysis_version: 2` 已经和成功那次一样，不是它们导致失败。

3.3.0 的技能没有讨论这些可选参数，模型就只提交了它要用的三个字段。后面的版本把工具参数写成了说明，模型开始按整张参数表填，空字段也被带上。3.3.5 已经把这些字样从技能里删掉了，但你贴出的这次调用仍然是七项，而且是报错之前的第一次调用就这样。所以现在多出来的空字段不是 Atlas 加上的，是这个 Agent 在组 MCP 请求时填上的。

不改 Open Atlas 时，能恢复的条件只有一个：让发出去的 JSON 重新变成上面那三项。请用一份全新对话再跑；当前对话里已经有多次七字段的失败调用，模型会照着抄。若新对话的第一笔 `analyze_result` 仍然带 `"query": ""` 和 `"result_handles": []`，要改的是 Agent 平台上 ETA-PastDue-Graph 的工具调用方式，让它不要提交未填写的参数。Atlas 服务端可以保持不动。

**User**

这么改有什么风险

---

**Cursor**

只去掉未填写的空参数，日常监控和报告那条已经跑通的路径风险很小。风险主要来自改得太宽，或者改到了别的工具上。

安全的改法是：字符串是 `""` 就不提交，数组是 `[]` 就不提交。`analyze_result` 里因此被拿掉的是空的 `query` 和空的 `result_handles`。句柄、SQL 和 `analysis_version: 2` 都还有值，请求会变回 15:54 那次被接受的三字段调用。`execute_eql` 的 `query` 有内容，不受影响。

需要避免的是这几类扩大：

- 不要把 `false`、`0` 或 `analysis_version` 一并删掉。`analysis_version` 一旦被丢掉，服务端按版本 1 处理，数量和比率会变成文本，后面的比较可能读错。`infer_time: false` 和 `input_row_limit: 20000` 留着没有关系，它们不是这次报错的原因。
- 不要把空的 `sql` 也删掉。`sql` 为空时，这个接口会改成只返回表结构，不返回指标行。调用看起来成功，日报却没有数。
- 若这个规则套在同一连接上的所有工具，别的工具可能真的用空字符串或空数组表示“全部”或“清空”。那种工具的结果会变。规则最好只加在 `analyze_result` 上。
- `query` 或 `result_handles` 里有实际内容时必须原样提交。否则按 EQL 直接分析、以及一次读多个句柄，这两条仍合法的用法会失效。现在的日报不走这两条。

这次修改不会核对句柄是否过期，也不会处理 Country Control 总量是 0 的对账差异。那两件事本来就独立于这次参数错误。
